# ==============================================================================
#  柳德米拉的主席办公室 —— 对话引擎
# ==============================================================================
#
#  【加一条台词要改哪里】（三步，都在本文件与 scripted_loc 里）
#   1. 本文件 REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line：给这条台词的 node 号加一个 if/else_if 块，
#      写好立绘差分 REU_ludmila_sprite、主角是否入画 REU_ludmila_prota（0/1）、
#      下一条 REU_ludmila_next、选项组 REU_ludmila_choice_group。
#   2. common/scripted_localisation/REU_Ludmila_Office_scripted_loc.txt 的 REU_GetLudmilaText：
#      加一条 text 块，把这个 node 指向本地化 key。
#   3. localisation 里补上那条 key 的中文。
#
#  【node 号规则】
#   100~ 顺序主线；110+ 分支；800 间隙空闲台词；900+ 互动/国策插入的段落。
#   某个 node 的 REU_ludmila_next = 0 表示「本章说完」→ 自动进入间隙。
#   见面章节 100~104：空镜（她还没出现）→ 她应门 → 主角入画 → 特写 → 选项。
#
#  【回忆录章节编号】每章 300 个号（够 20 条台词 + 3 组选项），决议点哪章就跳哪章的起始号
#   1095 → 1000   1096 → 1300   1097 → 1600   1098 → 1900
#   1099 → 2200   1100 → 2500   1101 → 2800   1102 → 3100
#   （每章内部再按 1000/1030/1040… 自由分段；整章说完的那句 next = 0）
#
#  【章节与间隙】REU_ludmila_chapter_state
#   0 = 本章剧情推进中（面板只有对话）
#   1 = 本章已结束、下一章未开 = 间隙（面板右侧「互动栏」开放：送巧克力 / 处理文件 / 看雪）
#   进入间隙：主线说完（见 REU_Ludmila_On_Session_End）
#   离开间隙：点任意处继续（node 800 的 next 指向下一章首节点），或国策调用 REU_Ludmila_Start_Chapter
#
#  【对外接口】
#   REU_Ludmila_Add_Affection  : 先 set_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta = N }（可为负），自动结算爱心/阶段/阈值事件
#   REU_Ludmila_Unlock         : 解锁机制（幂等，开局由 on_actions 调一次）
#   REU_Ludmila_Open / Close   : 开关全屏面板
#   REU_Ludmila_Play_Chapter   : 决议/国策翻开一章回忆 —— 先 set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 该章首节点 }，再调本效果
#   REU_Ludmila_Start_Chapter  : 结束间隙、开启新章节（调用前先把 node 设成新章节首节点）
#   REU_Ludmila_Gift_Chocolate / _Paperwork / _Snow : 互动，面板右侧按钮调用
# ==============================================================================


# ---------- 初始化：保证所有变量存在（新档 / 老档 / AI 都安全） ----------
REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 100 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_next > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 100 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
	}
	# 主角（心腹）立绘：0 = 不出现，1 = 出现
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 0 }
	}
	# 当前说话人：0 = 旁白，1 = 柳德米拉，2 = 主角。名牌与非说话者压暗都由它驱动
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 0 }
	}
	# 场景层底图：0 = 主席办公室，1 = 1095 谷仓，2 = 1096 麦田，3 = 1097 移动城市下层区，4 = 1098 工人夜校，5 = 1099 第一丝织品制造厂，6 = 1100 圣骏堡公爵宅邸宴会厅，7 = 1101 苏维埃宫会议室，8 = 1102 情报局局长办公室，9/10/11 = 1102 冬 密室/顶层走廊/塔露拉的办公室（同图不同地点标题）
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_scene > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_scene = 0 }
	}
	# 0 = 本章剧情推进中；1 = 本章已结束、下一章未开（间隙，右侧互动栏开放）
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_heart_frame > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_heart_frame = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_stage > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_stage = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta > -0.5 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta = 0 }
	}
}


# ---------- 解锁机制 ----------
REU_Ludmila_Unlock = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = REU_ludmila_unlocked } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 100 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_scene = 0 }
		REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
		set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_unlocked
		country_event = { id = reu_ludmila.1 days = 1 }
	}
}

# ---------- 开启新章节（结束间隙） ----------
# 用法：国策 / 事件里先 set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 新章节首节点 }，再调用本效果
REU_Ludmila_Start_Chapter = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_scene = 0 }
	REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
}


# ---------- 开关面板 ----------
# 不检查 REU_ludmila_unlocked：回忆录决议开局即可点，旗标只用来记「解锁通知已发过」
REU_Ludmila_Open = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
	set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_office_open
}

REU_Ludmila_Close = {
	clr_country_flag = REU_ludmila_office_open
}


# ---------- 点「继续」推进一句 ----------
REU_Ludmila_Advance_Click = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 0 compare = equals } }
			REU_Ludmila_Close = yes
			REU_Ludmila_On_Session_End = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 0 compare = equals } } }
			# 从间隙走进下一章时收起互动栏；本章内推进时这行没有副作用
			set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
			set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = REU_ludmila_next }
			REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
		}
	}
}


# ---------- 台词表：node -> 立绘 / 说话人 / 下一条 / 选项组 ----------
# 立绘索引见 scripted_loc 的 REU_GetLudmilaSprite：
#   6 正装全身  7 正装特写  8 休闲全身  9 休闲特写  10 空镜（她还没出现）
# REU_ludmila_speaker：0 旁白 / 1 她 / 2 主角 —— 名牌文字与非说话者的压暗立绘都看它
REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 100 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 10 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_scene = 0 }

		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 101 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 101 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 6 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 102 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 102 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 6 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 2 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 103 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 103 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 7 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 104 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 104 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 7 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 110 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 7 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 114 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 111 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 6 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 114 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 112 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 7 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 114 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 113 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 6 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 114 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 114 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 6 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 0 }
	}
	# 间隙状态（本章已结束、下一章未开）：她换了休闲装，next 指向下一章首节点，正式版由国策改写
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 800 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 8 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 100 }
	}
	# 互动反应段落：说完 next = 0 会自动回到间隙
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 901 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 9 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 902 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 9 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 900 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 8 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 0 }
	}
	# ===== 回忆录 1095：雪夜谷仓与第一个贪污犯（章节起始号 1000，场景 1 = 谷仓） =====
	# 首节点自己把 scene 切到 1；整章说完 next = 0 → On_Session_End 再把 scene 拉回 0 的办公室
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 1000 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_scene = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 10 }

		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_sprite = 8 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_prota = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_speaker = 0 }

		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }

		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_choice_group = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_next = 1004 }
	}
	else_if = {

# ---------- 好感度结算（对外接口） ----------
# 用法：先 set_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta = N }，再 REU_Ludmila_Add_Affection = yes
REU_Ludmila_Add_Affection = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	add_to_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection = REU_ludmila_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = REU_ludmila_affection min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta = 0 }
	REU_Ludmila_Refresh_Hearts = yes

	# 阈值事件：每个阶段只在首次跨越时触发一次（每档独立判断，避免连跳漏发）
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 24.9 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_1 }
		}
		set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_1
		country_event = { id = reu_ludmila.3 days = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 49.9 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_2 }
		}
		set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_2
		country_event = { id = reu_ludmila.4 days = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 74.9 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_3 }
		}
		set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_3
		country_event = { id = reu_ludmila.5 days = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 99.9 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_4 }
		}
		set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_tier_4
		country_event = { id = reu_ludmila.6 days = 1 }
	}

	# 好感 100：见证索科洛夫与柳德米拉的婚礼（只触发一次）
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 99.9 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = REU_ludmila_wedding_done }
		}
		set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_wedding_done
		country_event = { id = reu_ludmila.7 days = 1 }
	}
}

# 爱心格 0~4 与关系阶段 0~4
REU_Ludmila_Refresh_Hearts = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 24.9 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_heart_frame = 0 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_stage = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 49.9 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_heart_frame = 1 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_stage = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 74.9 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_heart_frame = 2 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_stage = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { REU_ludmila_affection > 99.9 } } }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_heart_frame = 3 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_stage = 3 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_heart_frame = 4 }
		set_variable = { REU_ludmila_stage = 4 }
	}
}


# ---------- 本章说完 → 进入「间隙」 ----------
REU_Ludmila_On_Session_End = {
	set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_talked_today
	# 间隙：正文停在 node 800 的空闲台词上，右侧互动栏开放，直到下一章被 REU_Ludmila_Start_Chapter 开启
	# 间隙一律在办公室（回忆/闪回结束后也由图收尾回到这里）
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 1 }
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_scene = 0 }
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 800 }
	REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
}


# ==============================================================================
#  回忆章节入口：决议【我和柳德米拉的回忆录】的每一条都调它
#  用法：先 set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 该章首节点 }，再 REU_Ludmila_Play_Chapter = yes
#  场景 / 立绘 / 说话人由该 node 自己的台词块决定（见 REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line）
#  整章说完那一句 next = 0 → 自动回办公室间隙
# ==============================================================================
REU_Ludmila_Play_Chapter = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
	REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
	REU_Ludmila_Open = yes
}


# ==============================================================================
#  政变成功：柳德米拉接管政权（1102 年冬那章的末句调用）
#  ⚠️ 换在任领袖要两步：① add_country_leader_role 补角色 ② retire_country_leader 退掉在任者 + promote_character 推她。
#    只做①（哪怕带 promote_leader = yes）在实测里**不会**换人 —— 塔露拉会留在领袖位上。
#  这里不动执政党本身（仍是 despotism），只把党魁换成她 —— 剧情上她是软禁塔露拉后接手的。
#  上台后置 REU_ludmila_in_power 旗标，方便国策/事件拿去当解锁条件。
# ==============================================================================
REU_Ludmila_Coup_Take_Power = {
	# 1) 给她补一个「执政党子意识形态」的领袖角色 —— 只加一次
	#    （她原本只有 bolshevism_subtype 的领袖角色，而 REU 执政党是 despotism / 塔露拉走 resistance_group_subtype）
	#    ⚠️ 重复调用会报 "Character already has a country leader role for ideology"，所以必须带旗标
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = REU_ludmila_in_power } }
		set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_in_power
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = REU_Lyudmila
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = resistance_group_subtype
				traits = { REU_crownslayer REU_crownslayer_fame }
			}
		}
	}
	# 2) 关键一步：光 add_country_leader_role + promote_leader **推不动在任的那位**（实测塔露拉会留在位上）。
	#    必须先把在任领袖退职，再 promote_character 推她上党魁 —— 这是 vanilla（AAT_Denmark）与本 mod
	#    events/CLB.txt 都在用的两步写法。带「已经是她就跳过」的判断，重复执行安全。
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = REU_Lyudmila ruling_only = yes } } }
		retire_country_leader = yes
	}
	promote_character = {
		character = REU_Lyudmila
		ideology = resistance_group_subtype
	}
}


# ==============================================================================
#  互动（决议与面板右侧按钮共用，别把逻辑写两遍）
#  PP 花费不在这里：决议用 cost，面板按钮在 scripted_gui 里 add_political_power
# ==============================================================================

# 送她一包巧克力
REU_Ludmila_Gift_Chocolate = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_used_chocolate
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta = 6 }
	REU_Ludmila_Add_Affection = yes
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 901 }
	REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
	REU_Ludmila_Open = yes
}

# 替她处理一天的文件
REU_Ludmila_Gift_Paperwork = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_used_paperwork
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta = 4 }
	REU_Ludmila_Add_Affection = yes
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 900 }
	REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
	REU_Ludmila_Open = yes
}

# 陪她在窗边看一会儿雪
REU_Ludmila_Gift_Snow = {
	REU_Ludmila_Init_Variables = yes
	set_country_flag = REU_ludmila_used_snow
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_delta = 3 }
	REU_Ludmila_Add_Affection = yes
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_chapter_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { REU_ludmila_node = 902 }
	REU_Ludmila_Resolve_Line = yes
	REU_Ludmila_Open = yes
}
